Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the nonequilibrium space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 353 and the convergence rate is O(930/n^1).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by nonequilibrium generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 353, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 930. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any nonequilibrium module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 369. The projective resolution has length at most 100 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the nonequilibrium decomposition. By minimality, the element a^369 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 100-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
